# DECISIONS.md

Numbered decision records. Status values are **Proposed**, **Accepted** (owner-approved or minor/autonomous), **Superseded** and **Rejected**. Never delete a record; supersede it.

| ID | Date | Decision | Status | Rationale / Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D001 | 2026-09-27 | Use QuantConnect Cloud (LEAN + AlgoSeek prices + Security Master + Morningstar fundamentals) as the single data source and engine for Phase 1, driven via the REST API | Accepted (owner, CP1) | Survivorship-free, point-in-time market cap from 1998; realistic execution; about $10–$100/month; no local data to manage. See CP1 §1 and §2.7. |
| D002 | 2026-09-27 | No second, fast research engine initially; measure throughput at CP2 and propose one only if it is a bottleneck | Accepted | Owner instruction: build only what is demonstrably needed. |
| D003 | 2026-09-27 | Universe is defined by point-in-time MarketCap ≥ $2B nominal, not by index membership | Accepted (owner, CP1) | Avoids needing a paid historical-constituents dataset and index-committee effects; matches the owner's spec. |
| D004 | 2026-09-27 | Data split: IS 1999-01-04 → 2014-12-31; VAL 2015-01-01 → 2021-12-31; HOLDOUT 2022-01-01 → 2026-08-31 (locked in code) | Superseded by D033/D034 (2026-09-28) | See CP1 §6. The alternative (2022 moved into VAL) was considered. |
| D005 | 2026-09-27 | Execution: signal on the T close, market-on-open fill on T+1; raw prices for fills, adjusted prices for signals | Accepted | Required for realism; no fills at the signal bar's close. |
| D006 | 2026-09-27 | Metrics computed locally with one shared module from the QC equity curve and trade list | Accepted | Consistency across strategies; independent of QC summary statistics. |
| D007 | 2026-09-27 | Storage is plain files in Git (Markdown, CSV, JSON, gzip); no database; no raw data in Git | Accepted | Simplicity; QC's licence forbids raw-data export. |
| D008 | 2026-09-27 | Official experiments run only from a clean git tree and record commit, params, dates, QC backtest ID, LEAN version and result hashes | Accepted | Reproducibility requirement. |
| D009 | 2026-09-27 | Checkpoints are approved by merging the checkpoint branch into `main` through a PR | Accepted (owner, CP1) | Gives a durable, auditable approval record. |
| D010 | 2026-09-27 | Assumed account size for cost modelling: $100,000 | Accepted (owner, CP1) | Affects only minimum-commission effects. |
| D011 | 2026-09-27 | QuantConnect subscription: Researcher seat ($10/month) plus B2-8 backtest node ($14/month), $24/month total | Accepted (owner) | Owner purchased. Within the budget target. |
| D012 | 2026-09-27 | Git: all checkpoint work is pushed; the owner approves checkpoints by merging into `main` through a PR | Accepted (owner, CP1) | Confirms D009. GitHub remains the permanent source of truth. |
| D013 | 2026-09-27 | Result channel from QC: the harness plots a daily custom chart `QR` (equity, cash, positions, eligible count); fills come from the orders API; small `QR*` log lines carry split events and a run summary | Accepted (minor) | ObjectStore export is blocked for non-Institutional accounts; logs are capped at 100 KB per backtest. Keep total custom series ≤ 10 per algorithm (QC silently dropped a chart when an algorithm had 14). |
| D014 | 2026-09-27 | Strategy number ranges: S000 = pipeline demo, S001–S899 = research, B900–B949 = benchmarks, X950–X999 = infrastructure. Experiment `E###-##` uses the strategy number, so IDs never collide | Accepted (minor) | Enforced by `experiment.validate`. |
| D015 | 2026-09-27 | "No leverage" is enforced by the harness and verified, not by LEAN's cash-account model. LEAN runs with the IB brokerage model, margin account type and buying-power checks disabled. The harness plans cash for every batch of orders with a 2% buffer; a run fails integrity if cash drops below −1% of equity at any close, and a warning is raised below 0 | Accepted (minor) | Market-on-open sells and buys fill together. LEAN's check at submission would reject buys funded by same-batch sells, and a cash account adds settlement delays not relevant to the research question. Refines RESEARCH_PLAN §4 "cash account". |
| D016 | 2026-09-27 | Prices: all subscriptions are RAW, so fills, commissions and dividends as cash are realistic. Signals use harness-maintained split- and dividend-adjusted rolling windows, warmed up with `SCALED_RAW` history, which is point-in-time. `ADJUSTED` mode is never used | Accepted (minor) | `ADJUSTED` levels embed future corporate actions, a look-ahead for any price-level rule (e.g. price > $5). |
| D017 | 2026-09-27 | Structural universe filter: Morningstar security type ST00000001 (common stock), primary share, not a depositary receipt, exchange NYS/NAS/ASE. Eligibility on day T uses fundamentals as of the T−1 close | Accepted (minor) | Matches the owner's "US common stocks"; ADRs and OTC stocks excluded as planned at CP1. |
| D018 | 2026-09-27 | Metric conventions: Sharpe and Sortino with rf = 0, 252 days; trade PnL = price PnL net of fees (dividends excluded); portfolio metrics include dividends; DSR trial count = every original run of kind research or demo | Accepted (minor) | Simple and consistent. Benchmarks use the same code, so comparisons are like for like. |
| D019 | 2026-09-27 | Equal-weight benchmark B901: monthly rebalance to equal weights across all eligible names, 25% tolerance band, $10M notional | Accepted (minor) | A $100K account cannot hold about 1,000 names without minimum-commission distortion. The band avoids thousands of tiny drift trades. |
| D020 | 2026-09-27 | Scratch runs (`--scratch`) are allowed only for infrastructure, benchmark or demo code, never for research strategies. They are not registered, but the log records how many were made | Accepted (minor) | Debugging the harness must not create un-logged research trials. |
| D021 | 2026-09-27 | Every experiment is pinned to an explicit LEAN build (`lean_version_id`); the runner verifies the build that actually ran. CP2 runs use **18131**, the branch that carries the **new** Morningstar dataset | Accepted (minor) | QC switches master to the new dataset on 2026-10-10 and retires the old dataset on 2026-10-31, so results on the old one would become unreproducible. E951-01 is the only run on the old dataset (master 18130); it is kept as a record. |
| D022 | 2026-09-27 | The structural exchange filter accepts both the old (NYS, ASE) and the new (NYSE, AMEX) Morningstar exchange codes, plus NAS | Accepted (minor) | The new dataset renamed the codes. Found by probe; E951-02 undercounted because of it and is superseded by E951-03. |
| D023 | 2026-09-27 | Tradability filters: raw price ≥ $5 and 20-day average dollar volume ≥ $5M, as of the prior close | Accepted (owner, 2026-09-28) | Used provisionally by the CP2 benchmark and demo runs only. |
| D024 | 2026-09-27 | Base slippage 10 bps per side; stress 20/40/60 bps; IB fixed commissions | Accepted (owner, 2026-09-28) | Conservative for opening-auction fills with small orders; covers pre-decimalization spreads. |
| D025 | 2026-09-27 | Portfolio constraints: long-only, no leverage, ≤ 10% per position at entry, ≤ 20 positions, 2% cash buffer, minimum position $2,000 | Accepted (owner, 2026-09-28), as amended by D041 | |
| D026 | 2026-09-27 | Screening, robustness and validation gates as in CP2 report §6 (incl. DSR ≥ 0.90 and PBO ≤ 0.30) | Superseded by D036 (adjusted for the 2010+ history) | To be fixed before any research data is examined. |
| D027 | 2026-09-27 | Universe data remedy: ask QuantConnect about pre-2009 MarketCap (option D); build and test a survivorship-free size proxy calibrated on 2010–2014 (option B); fallback is option A (MarketCap from 2010 with a re-split) | Superseded by D033 (option B tested, then rejected by the owner) | CP2 data audit: the old dataset omits all later-failed companies; the new dataset has almost no MarketCap before 2009. |
| D028 | 2026-09-27 | CP2 infrastructure APPROVED by the owner. The owner declined to contact QuantConnect support (option D dropped) and authorised the size-proxy evaluation (option B) as a CP2 addendum at $0. No research campaign until the owner approves the proxy result | Accepted (owner) | Plan pre-registered in `docs/data/size_proxy_plan.md` before any result. |
| D029 | 2026-09-27 | Harness fix: NaN daily dollar volumes are skipped, and the ADV test is written so that NaN can never pass | Accepted (minor) | The new dataset has some NaN volumes in 2020–21. | D030 | 2026-09-27 | Structural "US common stock" rule changed from "common, not ADR, primary share" to "common, not ADR, and (primary share OR US-domiciled company)"; implemented once as `is_us_common()` in the harness | Accepted (minor, correction; reported to the owner) | Probe: in 2012, 187 common stocks ≥ $2B on the new dataset were flagged non-primary, including GE, BAC, V, SPG, EXC, CMCSA, DELL, YHOO and MON, so the D017 rule silently excluded major US companies. | D031 | 2026-09-28 | Size proxy "C20 + E5" (ADV63 ≥ $20M; drop known non-US-common types; drop no-fundamentals securities with a 0.95 return twin) proposed as the survivorship-free research universe, with limitations | Rejected (owner, D033) | Provisional; `docs/data/size_proxy_evaluation.md`. A shorter, faithful period is preferred to a longer one with a different universe definition. |

| D039 | 2026-09-28 | Commission model: **$7 per executed order**, buy or sell ($14 per normal round trip), replacing IB per-share pricing. An order filled in pieces is charged once; separate orders are each charged. Slippage stays separate (10 bps per side, proposed D024). Implemented as `FixedPerOrderFeeModel` in the harness; required by config validation for every 2010-scheme experiment; verified per run by the `commission_fixed_per_order` integrity check. Harness cash planning includes the fee on buys and sells | Accepted (owner) | Owner's actual brokerage. Pre-D039 configs (no `commission_per_order`) keep the IB model so they stay reproducible as history. All metrics are computed from the net equity curve and net trade PnL. |
| D040 | 2026-09-28 | Runner pre-flight: refuse to start (unregistered) if QuantConnect's daily log allowance is below 300 KB | Superseded by D046 | E953-03 was lost because the allowance ran out mid-run. |
| D041 | 2026-09-28 | Minimum position size: recommend raising from $2,000 to $5,000, with a maximum of 15 positions (≈ $6.5K each at $100K) | Accepted (owner, 2026-09-28) | At $14 per round trip, $2,000 costs 0.70% + 0.20% slippage = 0.90% per trade; $5,000 costs 0.48%. See the CP2 amendment §6a. |
| D042 | 2026-09-28 | **Validation stays out-of-sample.** Promotion to VAL freezes the strategy's code and parameters (a promotion record with code and parameter hashes). A VAL or WF run is refused unless the files match the record exactly. Each strategy lineage gets at most one VAL run. VAL results may only accept or reject: a strategy changed after seeing 2018–21 cannot be re-run on VAL as if out-of-sample | Accepted (owner); enforced in code | Owner requirement 1. |
| D043 | 2026-09-28 | Quantify the post-2010 survivorship gap (missing fundamentals for later-ended securities) by year, with examples, price-data presence and bias direction. Every research report discloses it | Accepted (owner) | Owner requirement 2. |
| D044 | 2026-09-28 | $100,000 remains the primary research account. The harness makes position limits account-size aware: minimum position $5,000 (absolute), and effective maximum positions = min(15, ⌊equity × (1 − buffer) / $5,000⌋). Finalists get account-size re-tests via configs that differ only in `cash` (kind research, `account_size_test_of`); these are not selection trials | Accepted (owner) | Owner requirement 3; fixed $7 commissions make economics size-dependent. |
| D045 | 2026-09-28 | Research Cycle 1 (C01) is authorised once the D039 on-QuantConnect verification (E950-03, E900-03, E901-02) passes. If any fails: stop, diagnose, fix, re-verify, report. The holdout is never accessed | Accepted (owner) | Owner requirement 4. |
| D046 | 2026-09-28 | **No dependency on QuantConnect logs.** The harness publishes its results (run summary, fill self-check, split and delisting events, canary, corporate-action and audit outputs) as **summary statistics**, read through `backtests/read`. That channel was probed and returns all 65 keys and 200,000-character values intact; runtime statistics were rejected because they cut values at 200 characters and dropped 16 of 60 keys. Fills, fees ($7 check via each OrderEvent's `orderFeeAmount`), tags and timing come from the Orders API; equity from the custom chart; a new check compares chart days with QuantConnect's `tradeableDates`. Logs are read only when reproducing runs made with the pre-D046 harness | Accepted (owner instruction) | The daily log quota (≈ 3 MB) had blocked verification for ~13 hours. No check was removed or weakened; one was added. Supersedes D040 (log pre-flight). |
| D047 | 2026-09-28 | `--reproduce` rebuilds project files from the **original run's commit** (it used HEAD before) | Accepted (minor, fix) | Found while reviewing D046. A reproduction must use the code that produced the original. |
| D048 | 2026-09-28 | Claude merges its own work into `main` (through a PR, for an audit trail) after pushing, instead of waiting for the owner to merge. Checkpoint STOPs and owner approvals are unchanged | Accepted (owner) | Owner instruction: "from next time I want you to merge the commit yourself". Supersedes the merge mechanics of D009/D012. |
| D049 | 2026-09-28 | **Forced (delisting) liquidations are charged $7.** LEAN executes them with a $0 fee, bypassing the fee model, so the harness debits $7 from cash once per such order and records it (QRFORCEDFEE). Local fills mirror the debit, so trade PnL and the "$7 per executed order" check cover them | Accepted (minor, conservative) | Found by the D039 verification: E901-02 had 465 delisting liquidations at $0. Owner rule: $7 for every executed sell order. For merger cash-outs a real broker may charge nothing, so this errs conservative. Verified on QuantConnect (scratch X952: 4 of 4 debited). |
| D050 | 2026-09-28 | **Downloads must be verifiably complete.** The runner waits until the downloaded distinct orders equal QuantConnect's "Total Orders". New integrity checks: `orders_download_complete`, `fills_match_harness_count`. The commission check is never skipped (an empty fill list passes only if the harness recorded zero fills) | Accepted (minor, fix) | E901-02: the first orders read returned 0 orders, and the run looked clean, with the commission check silently absent. The verification gate caught it. |
| D051 | 2026-09-28 | **No-borrowing execution model (global).** Buys are funded only by cash already held; proceeds of same-batch sells, and the slots those sells free, become usable only after the sells have executed. Planned buys × (1 + 15% gap reserve) + fees must fit within cash − 2% × equity. Sells cancelled by LEAN on ticker changes are resubmitted at the next close. Integrity now fails on **any** negative cash (the old tolerance was −1%). Versioned as `execution_model: d051`; new research runs must use it; older configs are history (`d044`) | Accepted (owner instruction; diagnosis in `research/cycles/C01_incidents.md`) | Confirmed causes: (A) LEAN cancelled sells on ticker changes (MATX, E004-01; MDLZ, E005-02) while their funded buys executed; (B) opening gaps of +5.5% to +12.9% on 2016-11-30 buys (E004-02). All 19 C01 variations, both benchmarks and the canary are re-run under D051 with new IDs; the originals stay in the registry with annotation rows. |
| D052 | 2026-09-28 | Runner resilience: wait up to 2 hours for QuantConnect to publish order events (E002-03: the events arrived later, complete); recompile once on "Compile id not found" (E003-03); S004 keeps 210 bars (E004-04 bug) | Accepted (minor, fixes) | See `C01_incidents.md`. |
| D053 | 2026-09-29 | Runner resilience II (E003-04/05/06 incidents): (1) orders download retries transient API errors and `loading` replies within its 2 h window, still accepting only a count-complete download with every fill event; (2) a backtest with no progress for 45 min is declared stalled (was: waited 6 h); (3) failed runs record QC project/backtest ID, start time and failure stage; (4) pre-flight refuses to start (unregistered) while any backtest is running; (5) attempts in which no backtest ran are annotated `not_started` and excluded from the trial count; stalled backtests are never deleted without owner approval | Accepted (minor, fix; `not_started` rule per owner 2026-09-29; E003-06 deletion owner-approved) | Diagnosis in `research/cycles/C01_incidents.md` §6: transient QC outage 16:01–17:12 UTC, not S003-specific. |
| D054 | 2026-09-29 | Harness: any LEAN cancellation of a harness **sell** (ticker change or other) is re-issued at the next close, capped at the held quantity, except names with a delisting warning (LEAN liquidates those); cancelled buys are not re-issued; all cancellation counters are initialised and required. Verification trio and all 19 C01 variations re-run under new IDs (E950-06, E900-06, E901-05; E001-11..E005-12) so the final comparison uses one harness | Accepted (minor, bug fix) | The D051 handler matched on the event message, which LEAN leaves empty (it rewrites the order tag instead); found by the E004-09 order audit (`C01_incidents.md` §7). |
| D055 | 2026-09-29 | C01 robustness battery pre-declared (`research/cycles/C01_robustness_plan.md`, E005-13..27) before running; robustness runs are kind research, cycle `C01-R`, `robustness_of`, counted as trials and excluded from the variation table. PBO and DSR reported at CP3 as Validation-gate items; the PBO definition (CP2, ≤ 0 logit counts as overfit) is applied unchanged | Accepted (minor) | Owner decides at CP3 how PBO applies to H005 and whether to change the D051 cash handling. |

| D059 | 2026-09-29 | **Dead-position defect found, not fixed:** securities acquired without a delisting event in QuantConnect's data (OAK 2019-09-30, BPL 2019-11-01, and 7 others in the EW benchmark) leave harness sell orders 'submitted' forever and positions frozen at the last price. E005-28: about 11% of equity and 2 of 15 slots, late 2019 to 2021-12-31; E901-05: 9 positions of about 0.1%; no IS run affected (order audit of all final C01 runs). Proposed fix: integrity check (harness order open > 5 trading days fails the run) plus treating a security that stops delivering data as delisted. Awaits owner approval | Proposed | Found in the owner-requested E005-28 reconciliation (CP4 §4). |

| D061 | 2026-09-29 | **D057 fix (implemented):** `non_common_reason()` in the harness excludes, from the ≥ $2B universe, securities Morningstar labels common stock that are: partnership units (LP flag, or share class describing partnership units); LLC units (LLC flag *and* an LLC share class, or share class 'LLC … Class/Units'); investment vehicles (Morningstar industry template 'V': closed-end funds, BDCs); SIC 6726 / 6770 / 6792. Morningstar company flags describe CURRENT status, so the LLC flag alone is not used (it would delete acquired corporations such as VMware or Sprint from history). A dated override table corrects known cases (Yahoo until Altaba, Blackstone from 2019-07-01, Carlyle from 2020-01-01, Burford, ACAS). Evidence: probe E956-01; tests on 68 real classification records | Accepted (minor, bug fix; owner-directed) | Universe rule unchanged in intent: US operating-company common stock. |
| D062 | 2026-09-29 | **D059 fix (implemented):** the harness counts trading sessions (real SPY bars) and records each symbol's last real (not filled-forward) bar. A held position with no real bar for more than 10 sessions is taken out at its **last real close from the data stream** (as LEAN does for a delisting), charged $7, its open orders cancelled and never re-issued, and logged (QRSTALE); the local accounting mirrors it. If no real close is known the position is left and the run fails (`no_unresolved_stale_holdings`). New fail checks: `no_stale_open_orders` (any order still open > 10 days before the end, from the Orders API) and `harness_no_stale_open_orders`; each fallback exit makes the run `completed_with_warnings` (`stale_exits`) | Accepted (minor, bug fix; owner-directed) | No synthetic price is ever invented. |
| D063 | 2026-09-29 | **Harness window defect found in the C01 audit and fixed:** a symbol picked at the close that left the universe overnight lost its price window before its buy filled, so window-based exit rules never saw the holding. Fix: windows are kept while an order is pending; daily safety net reloads the point-in-time window for any holding without one (stat `windows_restored`, warn check). Affects all H001 runs (7-15 of 15 slots stranded; H001 conclusion INCONCLUSIVE); H002-H005 unaffected (liquidate-others or day-count exits). Unit-tested; no strategy re-run (owner: no new strategy backtests) | Accepted (minor, bug fix) | Found while auditing C01 for D059. |

Pushed history is not rewritten. Queues no longer run while code is edited | Accepted (minor) | Transparency. |

Lemma: a continuing non-event stock's null vector never changes because of other stocks. | D270 | 2026-10-20 | **UCR-1 implementation decisions (Claude, minor, before the freeze; within D269).** (1) **Point-in-time SEC object `rr/ucr_sec_v1.json`:** the SCA-1 object already in the Object Store (`rr/sca_sec_v1.json`) cannot meet D269 §2: its ticker evidence is stored as retrospective windows (first filing − 100 days … last filing + 100 days) and its non-common exclusions use the whole filing history (a later LP conversion excludes earlier years). A literal "existing object only" reading would therefore force a STOP. Instead the **same independently sourced public SEC cache** (XBRL RSS index, companyfacts; no new source, no network, no QuantConnect output) is re-laid out per periodic filing (`[filed, prefix, common_ok, shares, cover_end]` + float facts), so the in-cloud rule uses only filings filed strictly before each decision day. Builder `research/rr/universe_repair/build_ucr_sec.py`; SHA-256 ba3ab515… pinned in `qresearch.rr_ucr`; uploaded once, plain JSON (D257). The SCA-1 object stays unchanged and is used only to MEASURE (same instrument as RR5). (2) **Float age 650 days, not SCA-1's 456:** a returns-blind pre-freeze check showed that with 456 days only 427–511 of about 2,700 live registrants had a usable float at the October–December 2012 month-ends (December year-ends: the float is measured at the second fiscal quarter-end and first published in the next 10-K), against 2,476–2,489 with 650 days; 650 follows from the SEC deadlines (6 + 12 + 3 months). The frozen SCA-1 instrument keeps 456 days (disclosed limit: SCA-1 / RR5 confirmed counts are lower bounds from about October to January; Gap_max and therefore PASS are unaffected).
